# Sound and soundset index

Sound names and soundsets played.

**3,335 distinct values** across 1156 scripts. Sorted by how many scripts use each: widely-shared values first, one-off values last.

A value used by exactly one script is that script's own; a value used by hundreds is shared plumbing.
